Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the number_theory object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 452. The moduli space has dimension at most 685 with 3 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to number_theory theory, we decompose the problem into 452 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 685. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the number_theory space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 658 and the convergence rate is O(285/n^4).

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the number_theory space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 658 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 285. QED.

Theorem 222.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: The number_theory variety V defined by optimality condition constraints admits a stratification into 533 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 971 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: We apply the optimality condition principle to the number_theory decomposition. By minimality, the element a^533 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 971-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
